```python
"""
Hybrid full-budget optimizer for non-negative sequences minimizing
2*n*max(conv(a,a))/(sum(a))**2.

The program uses:
- adaptive mask inference for possible evaluator variants,
- log-sum-exp / active-set L-BFGS in a softmax reference coordinate system,
- Frank-Wolfe directions with multiplicative-weight zero-sum-game solving
  and occasional exact HiGHS LP directions,
- fast exact rank-1 transfer coordinate search,
- multi-length exploration and symmetric polish.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    import scipy.optimize
    import scipy.sparse
    SCIPY_OK = True
except Exception:
    SCIPY_OK = False

_HI_AVAILABLE = None


def highs_available():
    global _HI_AVAILABLE
    if _HI_AVAILABLE is None:
        ok = False
        if SCIPY_OK:
            try:
                res = scipy.optimize.linprog(
                    [-1.0],
                    bounds=[(0.0, 1.0)],
                    method="HiGHS",
                    options={"disp": False}
                )
                ok = bool(res.success)
            except Exception:
                ok = False
        _HI_AVAILABLE = ok
    return _HI_AVAILABLE


def nxt_pow2(x):
    x = int(x)
    if x <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (x - 1).bit_length()


def norm_sum1(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    np.maximum(arr, 0.0, out=arr)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s < 1e-15:
        return np.ones(arr.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(arr.size)
    return arr / s


def fft_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = nxt_pow2(L)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:L]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c) & (c > 0.0), c, 0.0)
    return c


def fft_conv2(a, b):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if b.size != n:
        b = np.resize(b, n)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = nxt_pow2(L)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    B = np.fft.rfft(b, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * B, N)[:L]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c) & (c > 0.0), c, 0.0)
    return c


def slide_dot(a, p):
    """
    For p_l over convolution lags, return_i = sum_l p_l a_{l-i} (valid only).
    This is the half-gradient of sum_l p_l (a*a)_l with respect to a_i.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if p.size != L:
        tmp = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(p.size, L)
        if m > 0:
            tmp[:m] = p[:m]
        p = tmp
    N = nxt_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], N)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
    h = np.fft.irfft(A * P, N)
    out = h[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out) & (out > 0.0), out, 0.0)
    return out


def score_c(c, n, idx):
    if c.size == 0:
        return 1e100
    if idx is None:
        vals = c
    else:
        if idx.size == 0:
            return 1e100
        if idx.size == c.size:
            vals = c
        else:
            vals = c[idx]
    if vals.size == 0:
        return 1e100
    mx = float(np.max(vals))
    if not math.isfinite(mx) or mx < 0.0:
        mx = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * mx)


def mask_to_idx(mask, n=None):
    if mask is None:
        return None
    arr = np.asarray(mask, dtype=bool).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    idx = np.flatnonzero(arr)
    if idx.size == 0:
        if n is not None:
            return np.arange(2 * int(n) - 1, dtype=np.int64)
        return np.array([], dtype=np.int64)
    return idx.astype(np.int64, copy=False)


def candidate_masks(n):
    L = 2 * n - 1
    res = [("full", None)]

    widths = [0, 1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 32, 64, 128]
    for w in widths:
        if w > max(1, n):
            continue
        m = np.ones(L, dtype=bool)
        lo = max(0, n - 1 - w)
        hi = min(L - 1, n + w)
        m[lo:hi + 1] = False
        if m.any():
            res.append((f"cw_{w}", m))

    ew = [0, 1, 2, 4, 8]
    for w in ew:
        m = np.ones(L, dtype=bool)
        if w == 0:
            m[0] = False
            m[-1] = False
        else:
            if 2 * (w + 1) <= L:
                m[:w + 1] = False
                m[L - w - 1:] = False
            else:
                continue
        if m.any():
            res.append((f"ew_{w}", m))

    if L > 6:
        m = np.ones(L, dtype=bool)
        w = 4
        if w < n:
            lo = max(0, n - 1 - w)
            hi = min(L - 1, n + w)
            m[lo:hi + 1] = False
        else:
            m[n - 1] = False
        m[:2] = False
        m[-2:] = False
        if m.any():
            res.append(("cw4ew1", m))

    m = np.ones(L, dtype=bool)
    m[n:] = False
    if m.any():
        res.append(("first_n", m))

    m = np.ones(L, dtype=bool)
    m[:n - 1] = False
    if m.any():
        res.append(("last_n", m))

    return res


def mask_from_name(n, name):
    L = 2 * int(n) - 1
    n = int(n)
    if name is None:
        return None
    if name == "full":
        return None

    if name.startswith("cw_"):
        try:
            w = int(name.split("_")[1])
        except Exception:
            w = 0
        m = np.ones(L, dtype=bool)
        lo = max(0, n - 1 - w)
        hi = min(L - 1, n + w)
        m[lo:hi + 1] = False
        return m

    if name.startswith("ew_"):
        try:
            w = int(name.split("_")[1])
        except Exception:
            w = 0
        m = np.ones(L, dtype=bool)
        if w == 0:
            m[0] = False
            m[-1] = False
        else:
            if 2 * (w + 1) <= L:
                m[:w + 1] = False
                m[L - w - 1:] = False
            else:
                m[0] = False
                m[-1] = False
        return m

    if name == "cw4ew1":
        m = np.ones(L, dtype=bool)
        w = 4
        if w < n:
            lo = max(0, n - 1 - w)
            hi = min(L - 1, n + w)
            m[lo:hi + 1] = False
        else:
            m[n - 1] = False
        m[:2] = False
        m[-2:] = False
        return m

    if name == "first_n":
        m = np.ones(L, dtype=bool)
        m[n:] = False
        return m

    if name == "last_n":
        m = np.ones(L, dtype=bool)
        m[:n - 1] = False
        return m

    m = np.ones(L, dtype=bool)
    m[n - 1] = False
    return m


def safe_eval(a):
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
        if arr.size == 0:
            return float("inf")
        arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(arr.sum())
        if s < 0.01:
            return float("inf")
        vals = [float(x) for x in arr]
        v = evaluate_sequence(vals)
        if v is None:
            return float("inf")
        v = float(v)
        if not math.isfinite(v):
            return float("inf")
        return v
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def infer_mask(a, verbose=True):
    arr = norm_sum1(a)
    n = arr.size
    c = fft_conv(arr)
    actual = safe_eval(arr)

    full_score = score_c(c, n, None)
    if math.isfinite(actual):
        best_name = "full"
        best_diff = abs(full_score - actual)
    else:
        best_name = "cw_0"
        best_diff = float("inf")

    if math.isfinite(actual):
        for name, m in candidate_masks(n):
            idx = mask_to_idx(m, n)
            sc = score_c(c, n, idx)
            if not math.isfinite(sc):
                continue
            diff = abs(sc - actual)
            if diff < best_diff - 1e-10:
                best_diff = diff
                best_name = name

    if verbose:
        print("mask inference actual=" + str(actual) +
              " full=" + str(full_score) +
              " chosen=" + str(best_name) +
              " diff=" + str(best_diff))

    if not math.isfinite(actual):
        return "cw_0", mask_from_name(n, "cw_0"), actual

    return best_name, mask_from_name(n, best_name), actual


def default_sine(n):
    n = int(max(2, n))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    a = np.sin(np.pi * x) ** 2
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a += 1e-8
    return norm_sum1(a)


def random_smooth(n, seed):
    n = int(max(2, n))
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    s = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for k in range(1, 9):
        amp = 1.0 / math.sqrt(float(k))
        s += amp * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0) * np.sin(
            2.0 * np.pi * float(k) * x / float(n) + rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        )
    a = np.exp(0.35 * s)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a += 1e-5
    return norm_sum1(a)


def comb_seed(n, frac, seed):
    n = int(max(2, n))
    M = int(max(2, min(n, frac * n)))
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    a = np.full(n, 1e-4, dtype=np.float64)
    if M < n:
        pos = np.sort(rng.choice(n, size=M, replace=False))
        a[pos] = 1.0
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    np.maximum(a, 0.0, out=a)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-15:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return a / s


def interpolate_sequence(a, new_n):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    new_n = int(max(1, new_n))
    if arr.size == 0:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / float(new_n)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    np.maximum(arr, 0.0, out=arr)
    if arr.size == new_n:
        return norm_sum1(arr)
    old_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, arr.size)
    new_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n)
    y = np.interp(new_x, old_x, arr)
    y = np.where(np.isfinite(y), y, 0.0)
    np.maximum(y, 0.0, out=y)
    s = float(y.sum())
    if s < 1e-15:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / float(new_n)
    return y / s


def symmetrize(a):
    arr = norm_sum1(a)
    if arr.size <= 1:
        return arr
    arr = 0.5 * (arr + arr[::-1])
    return norm_sum1(arr)


def perturb_mul(a, sigma, seed):
    arr = norm_sum1(a)
    n = arr.size
    if n == 0:
        return arr
    if n == 1:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    s = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for k in range(1, 9):
        s += (rng.normal(0.0, 1.0) / math.sqrt(float(k))) * np.sin(
            2.0 * np.pi * float(k) * x / float(n) + rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        )
    s -= float(s.mean())
    loga = np.log(np.maximum(arr, 1e-12))
    loga = loga - float(np.max(loga)) + sigma * s
    loga = np.clip(loga, -50.0, 50.0)
    out = np.exp(loga)
    return norm_sum1(out)


def lse_core(a, beta, fixed_idx):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    c = fft_conv(a)
    if n == 0:
        return 0.0, np.array([], dtype=np.float64)

    if fixed_idx is None:
        vals = c
        if vals.size == 0:
            return 0.0, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        mx = float(np.max(vals))
        x = beta * (vals - mx)
        x = np.clip(x, -700.0, 700.0)
        e = np.exp(x)
        se = float(e.sum())
        if not math.isfinite(se) or se <= 0.0:
            e = np.ones(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
            se = float(c.size)
        p = e / se
    else:
        idx = np.asarray(fixed_idx, dtype=np.int64)
        if idx.size == 0:
            return 0.0, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        vals = c[idx]
        if vals.size == 0:
            return 0.0, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        mx = float(np.max(vals))
        x = beta * (vals - mx)
        x = np.clip(x, -700.0, 700.0)
        e = np.exp(x)
        se = float(e.sum())
        if not math.isfinite(se) or se <= 0.0:
            e = np.ones(idx.size, dtype=np.float64)
            se = float(idx.size)
        p = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
        p[idx] = e / se

    f = mx + math.log(max(se, 1e-300)) / max(beta, 1e-12)
    grad = 2.0 * slide_dot(a, p)
    grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
    if not math.isfinite(f):
        f = 1e100
    return float(f), grad


def softmax_from_u(u):
    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64)
    n = u.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    u = np.where(np.isfinite(u), u, -700.0)
    umax = float(np.max(u))
    e = np.exp(u - umax)
    e = np.where(e > 0.0, e, 0.0)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return e / s


def from_u(v, ref):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    n = v.size + 1
    ref = int(ref)
    if not (0 <= ref < n):
        ref = n - 1
    u = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if ref > 0:
        u[:ref] = v[:ref]
    if ref < n - 1:
        u[ref + 1:] = v[ref:]
    return softmax_from_u(u)


def make_u(a, ref=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a) & (a > 0.0), a, 1e-300)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64), 0
    if ref is None:
        ref = int(np.argmax(a))
    ref = int(ref)
    if not (0 <= ref < n):
        ref = 0
    a_ref = max(float(a[ref]), 1e-300)
    loga = np.log(a) - math.log(a_ref)
    loga[ref] = 0.0
    loga = np.clip(loga, -700.0, 700.0)
    if ref == 0:
        v = loga[1:]
    elif ref == n - 1:
        v = loga[:-1]
    else:
        v = np.concatenate((loga[:ref], loga[ref + 1:]))
    return v, ref


def obj_lse_u(u, beta, fixed_idx, reg_u, ref):
    n = u.size + 1
    if n <= 1:
        return 1e100, np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(u)):
        z = np.zeros(max(0, u.size), dtype=np.float64)
        return 1e100, z

    a = from_u(u, ref)
    f, ga = lse_core(a, beta, fixed_idx)
    if not math.isfinite(f):
        f = 1e100
        ga = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    if reg_u > 0.0:
        f += 0.5 * reg_u * float(np.dot(u, u))

    mean = float(np.dot(a, ga))
    gu = a * (ga - mean)

    gv = np.empty(max(0, n - 1), dtype=np.float64)
    if ref > 0:
        gv[:ref] = gu[:ref]
    if ref < n - 1:
        gv[ref:] = gu[ref + 1:]

    if reg_u > 0.0:
        gv = gv + reg_u * u

    gv = np.where(np.isfinite(gv), gv, 0.0)
    return float(f), gv


def run_lbfegs(a0, beta, fixed_idx, maxfun, reg_u=1e-12, verbose=False):
    a = norm_sum1(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1 or maxfun <= 0 or not SCIPY_OK:
        return a

    v0, ref = make_u(a)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        res = scipy.optimize.minimize(
            obj_lse_u,
            v0,
            args=(beta, fixed_idx, reg_u, ref),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            options={
                "maxfun": int(maxfun),
                "maxiter": int(maxfun) + 50,
                "ftol": 1e-12,
                "gtol": 1e-6,
                "disp": False
            }
        )
        if res is not None and hasattr(res, "x"):
            vx = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            if vx.size == v0.size and np.all(np.isfinite(vx)):
                a_out = from_u(vx, ref)
                a_out = norm_sum1(a_out)
                if verbose:
                    print("    L-BFGS beta=" + str(beta) +
                          " n=" + str(n) +
                          " maxfun=" + str(int(maxfun)) +
                          " t=" + str(round(time.monotonic() - t0, 3)))
                return a_out
    except Exception as e:
        print("    L-BFGS exception: " + repr(e))

    if verbose:
        print("    L-BFGS fallback t=" + str(round(time.monotonic() - t0, 3)))
    return a


def estimate_lse_cost(a, fixed_idx):
    if not SCIPY_OK:
        return 0.25
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    lse_core(a, 30.0, fixed_idx)
    dt = time.monotonic() - t0
    if not math.isfinite(dt) or dt <= 0.0:
        dt = 0.05
    return float(max(0.015, 1.35 * dt))


def top_indices(c, mask_idx, top_count):
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.int64)
    if mask_idx is None:
        size = c.size
        if top_count is None or top_count >= size:
            return np.arange(size, dtype=np.int64)
        top_count = int(max(0, min(size, top_count)))
        if top_count <= 0:
            return np.array([], dtype=np.int64)
        return np.argsort(c, kind="mergesort")[::-1][:top_count].astype(np.int64, copy=False)

    if mask_idx.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.int64)
    if top_count is None or top_count >= mask_idx.size:
        return mask_idx.copy()
    top_count = int(max(0, min(mask_idx.size, top_count)))
    if top_count <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.int64)
    vals = c[mask_idx]
    sub = np.argsort(vals, kind="mergesort")[::-1][:top_count]
    return mask_idx[sub].astype(np.int64, copy=False)


def make_fixed_idx(c, mask_idx, top_count, keep_frac=0.8):
    L = c.size
    if mask_idx is None:
        total = L
        all_idx = None
    else:
        total = mask_idx.size
        all_idx = mask_idx

    if total <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.int64)

    if top_count is None or top_count >= total:
        fixed = all_idx
    else:
        fixed = top_indices(c, mask_idx, int(top_count))

    if keep_frac is not None and keep_frac < 1.0:
        if mask_idx is None:
            mx = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
            near = np.flatnonzero(c >= keep_frac * mx).astype(np.int64, copy=False)
            if fixed is None:
                return None
            if near.size:
                fixed = np.unique(np.concatenate((fixed, near)))
        else:
            vals = c[mask_idx]
            mx = float(vals.max()) if vals.size else 0.0
            near = mask_idx[vals >= keep_frac * mx].astype(np.int64, copy=False)
            if fixed is None:
                fixed = np.unique(np.concatenate((all_idx, near))) if near.size else all_idx.copy()
            elif near.size:
                fixed = np.unique(np.concatenate((fixed, near)))
            fixed = np.intersect1d(fixed, mask_idx).astype(np.int64, copy=False)

    if fixed is None and mask_idx is not None:
        fixed = all_idx.copy()

    return fixed


def lbfegs_schedule(a0, budget, mask_idx, verbose=True):
    a = norm_sum1(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1:
        return a
    L = 2 * n - 1
    c = fft_conv(a)
    P0 = score_c(c, n, mask_idx)
    best = a.copy()
    best_P = P0
    t0 = time.monotonic()

    top_est = int(max(80, min(1500, L // 10)))
    fixed_est = make_fixed_idx(c, mask_idx, top_est, 0.85)
    cost = estimate_lse_cost(a, fixed_est)

    schedules = [
        (6.0, 0.70, 0.90),
        (20.0, 0.35, 0.85),
        (60.0, 0.18, 0.80),
        (180.0, 0.09, 0.75),
        (600.0, 0.045, 0.70),
        (1800.0, 0.02, 0.65)
    ]

    per = int(max(50, min(520, int(budget / max(cost * len(schedules) * 1.5, 0.1)))))

    for si, (beta, frac, keep) in enumerate(schedules):
        if time.monotonic() - t0 > budget - 5:
            break
        c = fft_conv(a)
        top_count = int(min(L, max(40, int(L * frac))))
        fixed = make_fixed_idx(c, mask_idx, top_count, keep)
        a_new = run_lbfegs(a, beta, fixed, per, verbose=False if verbose else False)
        c_new = fft_conv(a_new)
        P = score_c(c_new, n, mask_idx)
        if verbose:
            print("  LB stage=" + str(si) +
                  " beta=" + str(beta) +
                  " proxy=" + str(P) +
                  " best=" + str(best_P))
        if math.isfinite(P) and P < best_P - 1e-12:
            best = a_new.copy()
            best_P = P
        if math.isfinite(P) and P <= best_P * 1.01 + 1e-12:
            a = a_new
        else:
            a = best.copy()

    return best


def game_direction(a, c, active_idx, iters=18, seed=0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    active_idx = np.asarray(active_idx, dtype=np.int64)
    n = a.size
    K = active_idx.size
    if K <= 0 or n <= 0:
        return None
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    vals = c[active_idx]
    mx = float(np.max(vals)) if vals.size else 0.0
    if mx <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(mx):
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)

    logits = np.log(np.maximum(vals, 1e-300) / mx)
    logits -= float(np.max(logits))
    logits += rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, K)

    for _ in range(max(1, int(iters))):
        zm = float(np.max(logits))
        lw = np.exp(logits - zm)
        lw = np.where(lw > 0.0, lw, 0.0)
        sw = float(lw.sum())
        if not math.isfinite(sw) or sw <= 0.0:
            lw = np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64)
            sw = float(K)
        lw = lw / sw

        p = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
        p[active_idx] = lw

        pressure = slide_dot(a, p)
        pmx = float(np.max(pressure)) if pressure.size else 0.0
        pmn = float(np.min(pressure)) if pressure.size else 0.0
        ptp = pmx - pmn
        if ptp > 1e-30 and math.isfinite(ptp):
            scaled = (pressure - pmn) / ptp
        else:
            scaled = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

        w = np.exp(-6.0 * scaled)
        w = np.where(np.isfinite(w) & (w > 0.0), w, 0.0)
        w += 1e-12
        w = w / float(w.sum())
        g = 0.98 * w + 0.02 * (np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n))
        g = np.where(np.isfinite(g) & (g > 0.0), g, 0.0)
        gs = float(g.sum())
        if gs <= 0.0:
            return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        g = g / gs

        h = fft_conv2(a, g)
        ha = h[active_idx]
        ha = np.where(np.isfinite(ha), ha, 0.0)
        hm = float(np.max(ha)) if ha.size else 0.0
        hn = float(np.min(ha)) if ha.size else 0.0
        htp = hm - hn
        if htp > 1e-30 and math.isfinite(htp):
            logits += (10.0 / htp) * (ha - float(np.mean(ha)))

        logits -= float(np.max(logits))
        logits = np.where(np.isfinite(logits), logits, 0.0)
        logits = np.clip(logits, -100.0, 100.0)

    return g


def lp_direction(a, active_idx, time_limit=2.5, max_k=180):
    if not SCIPY_OK or not highs_available():
        return None
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    active_idx = np.asarray(active_idx, dtype=np.int64)
    n = a.size
    if active_idx.size == 0 or n <= 1:
        return None
    if active_idx.size > max_k:
        active_idx = active_idx[:max_k]
    K = active_idx.size
    if K == 0:
        return None

    row_parts = []
    col_parts = []
    val_parts = []

    for r, k in enumerate(active_idx):
        k = int(k)
        d0 = max(0, k - n + 1)
        d1 = min(n - 1, k)
        if d1 < d0:
            continue
        ds = np.arange(d0, d1 + 1, dtype=np.int32)
        if ds.size == 0:
            continue
        row_parts.append(np.full(ds.size, r, dtype=np.int32))
        col_parts.append(ds)
        val_parts.append(a[k - ds].astype(np.float64, copy=False))

    if not row_parts:
        return None

    rows = np.concatenate(row_parts)
    cols = np.concatenate(col_parts)
    vals = np.concatenate(val_parts)

    A = scipy.sparse.csr_matrix((vals, (rows, cols)), shape=(K, n))
    neg = scipy.sparse.csc_matrix(-np.ones((K, 1), dtype=np.float64))
    A_ub = scipy.sparse.hstack((A, neg), format="csc")

    c_obj = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    c_obj[n] = 1.0

    A_eq = np.ones((1, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
    A_eq[0, n] = 0.0
    b_eq = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    b_ub = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)

    bounds = [(0.0, None) for _ in range(n)] + [(None, None)]

    try:
        res = scipy.optimize.linprog(
            c_obj,
            A_ub=A_ub,
            b_ub=b_ub,
            A_eq=A_eq,
            b_eq=b_eq,
            bounds=bounds,
            method="HiGHS",
            options={"disp": False, "time_limit": float(time_limit)}
        )
        if res is None or not getattr(res, "success", False):
            return None
        g = np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=np.float64)
        g = np.where(np.isfinite(g) & (g > 0.0), g, 0.0)
        gs = float(g.sum())
        if gs < 1e-15:
            return None
        return g / gs
    except Exception:
        return None


def line_search_mix(a, g, c, cg, fg, idx):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    g = np.asarray(g, dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    cg = np.asarray(cg, dtype=np.float64)
    fg = np.asarray(fg, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size

    p0 = score_c(c, n, idx)
    best_p = p0
    best_t = 0.0

    base_ts = [0.01, 0.02, 0.04, 0.08, 0.15, 0.25, 0.40, 0.60, 0.80, 1.00]
    for t in base_ts:
        one = 1.0 - t
        val = (one * one) * c + (2.0 * t * one) * fg + (t * t) * cg
        p = score_c(val, n, idx)
        if p < best_p - 1e-14:
            best_p = p
            best_t = t

    if best_t > 0.0:
        lo = max(0.0, best_t - 0.04)
        hi = min(1.0, best_t + 0.04)
        for i in range(9):
            t = lo + (hi - lo) * (float(i) / 8.0)
            one = 1.0 - t
            val = (one * one) * c + (2.0 * t * one) * fg + (t * t) * cg
            p = score_c(val, n, idx)
            if p < best_p - 1e-14:
                best_p = p
                best_t = t

    if best_t <= 0.0:
        return a.copy(), best_p, 0.0

    new_a = (1.0 - best_t) * a + best_t * g
    new_a = norm_sum1(new_a)
    return new_a, best_p, best_t


def fw_search(a0, budget, mask_idx, verbose=True):
    a = norm_sum1(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1:
        return a
    L = 2 * n - 1
    c = fft_conv(a)
    P = score_c(c, n, mask_idx)
    best = a.copy()
    best_P = P

    topK = int(max(60, min(1200, L // 10)))
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    t = 0
    no_improve = 0

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < budget - 2 and no_improve < 120:
        t += 1
        left = budget - (time.monotonic() - t0)
        if left < 12:
            break

        active = top_indices(c, mask_idx, topK)
        if active.size == 0:
            break

        g = None
        if (t % 10 == 0) and left > 60 and highs_available():
            g = lp_direction(a, active, time_limit=2.5, max_k=180)

        if g is None:
            g = game_direction(a, c, active, iters=18, seed=12345 + t)

        if g is None:
            break

        cg = fft_conv(g)
        fg = fft_conv2(a, g)
        new_a, new_P, alpha = line_search_mix(a, g, c, cg, fg, mask_idx)

        if alpha > 0.0 and new_P < P - 1e-13:
            a = norm_sum1(new_a)
            c = fft_conv(a)
            P = score_c(c, n, mask_idx)
            no_improve = 0
            if P < best_P - 1e-12:
                best = a.copy()
                best_P = P
                if verbose and (t <= 3 or t % 20 == 0):
                    print("    FW t=" + str(t) +
                          " score=" + str(P) +
                          " alpha=" + str(round(alpha, 4)) +
                          " topK=" + str(topK))
            if t > 0 and t % 35 == 0:
                topK = max(40, int(0.75 * topK))
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve % 8 == 0:
                a = perturb_mul(best, 0.02 + 0.004 * (no_improve // 8), seed=9973 + t)
                c = fft_conv(a)
                P = score_c(c, n, mask_idx)
                no_improve = 0
                if verbose and t % 30 == 0:
                    print("    FW stall t=" + str(t) + " proxy=" + str(P))

    return best


def apply_move(c, a, s, d, tmove):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    c2 = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    n = a.size
    s = int(s)
    d = int(d)

    c2[d:d + n] += 2.0 * tmove * a
    c2[s:s + n] -= 2.0 * tmove * a

    t2 = tmove * tmove
    if 2 * d < c2.size:
        c2[2 * d] += t2
    if 2 * s < c2.size:
        c2[2 * s] += t2
    if s + d < c2.size:
        c2[s + d] -= 2.0 * t2

    c2 = np.where(np.isfinite(c2), c2, 0.0)
    c2[c2 < 0.0] = 0.0
    return c2


def coordinate_polish(a0, budget, mask_idx, verbose=False):
    a = norm_sum1(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1:
        return a
    L = 2 * n - 1
    c = fft_conv(a)
    best = a.copy()
    best_P = score_c(c, n, mask_idx)

    Kp = int(max(200, min(2000, L // 8)))
    fracs = (0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.40, 0.70)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    stall = 0
    rnd = 0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(777)

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < budget - 2 and stall < 100:
        rnd += 1
        left = budget - (time.monotonic() - t0)
        if left < 4:
            break

        active = top_indices(c, mask_idx, Kp)
        if active.size == 0:
            break

        p = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        p[active] = 1.0 / float(active.size)
        pressure = slide_dot(a, p)
        local_P = score_c(c, n, active)

        src_count = min(7, n)
        dst_count = min(10, n)

        src_score = pressure * (1.0 + n * a * 0.02)
        dst_score = pressure * (1.0 + n * a * 0.002)

        if n > src_count:
            src_pos = np.argpartition(src_score, src_count - 1)[:src_count]
        else:
            src_pos = np.arange(n)
        if n > dst_count:
            dst_pos = np.argpartition(dst_score, dst_count - 1)[:dst_count]
        else:
            dst_pos = np.arange(n)

        if n > 1:
            extra_s = rng.integers(0, n, 4)
            extra_d = rng.integers(0, n, 4)
        else:
            extra_s = np.array([], dtype=np.int64)
            extra_d = np.array([], dtype=np.int64)

        src_set = np.unique(np.concatenate((src_pos, extra_s)))
        dst_set = np.unique(np.concatenate((dst_pos, extra_d)))

        best_move = None

        for s in src_set:
            s = int(s)
            if a[s] < 1e-10:
                continue
            for d in dst_set:
                d = int(d)
                if d == s:
                    continue
                for frac in fracs:
                    tmove = frac * float(a[s])
                    if tmove <= 1e-15:
                        continue
                    c2 = apply_move(c, a, s, d, tmove)
                    pl = score_c(c2, n, active)
                    if pl < local_P - 1e-13:
                        if best_move is None or pl < best_move[0]:
                            best_move = (pl, s, d, tmove)

        if best_move is None:
            for _ in range(15):
                s = int(rng.integers(n))
                d = int(rng.integers(n))
                if s == d or a[s] < 1e-10:
                    continue
                tmove = float(a[s]) * float(rng.uniform(0.01, 0.25))
                if tmove <= 1e-15:
                    continue
                c2 = apply_move(c, a, s, d, tmove)
                pl = score_c(c2, n, active)
                if pl < local_P - 1e-13:
                    best_move = (pl, s, d, tmove)
                    break

        if best_move is not None:
            pl, s, d, tmove = best_move
            a[s] -= tmove
            a[d] += tmove
            a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
            np.maximum(a, 0.0, out=a)
            ssum = float(a.sum())
            if ssum < 1e-15:
                break
            a = a / ssum
            c = fft_conv(a)
            full_P = score_c(c, n, mask_idx)
            if full_P < best_P - 1e-12:
                best_P = full_P
                best = a.copy()
            stall = 0
            if verbose and (rnd <= 3 or rnd % 25 == 0):
                print("    Coord rnd=" + str(rnd) + " score=" + str(best_P))
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall % 5 == 0:
                a = perturb_mul(best, 0.02, seed=555 + rnd)
                c = fft_conv(a)
                full_P = score_c(c, n, mask_idx)
                if full_P < best_P:
                    best_P = full_P
                    best = a.copy()
                stall = 0

    return best


def try_candidates(cands, mask_idx, best_seq, best_actual, best_proxy, label):
    for cand in cands:
        if cand is None:
            continue
        arr = norm_sum1(cand)
        if arr.size == 0:
            continue

        sc = safe_eval(arr)
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_actual - 1e-15:
            c = fft_conv(arr)
            p = score_c(c, arr.size, mask_idx)
            best_seq = arr.copy()
            best_actual = float(sc)
            best_proxy = float(p)
            print("NEW BEST [" + label + "] score=" + str(sc) +
                  " n=" + str(arr.size) +
                  " proxy=" + str(p))

        if arr.size > 2:
            arr2 = symmetrize(arr)
            sc2 = safe_eval(arr2)
            if math.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_actual - 1e-15:
                c2 = fft_conv(arr2)
                p2 = score_c(c2, arr2.size, mask_idx)
                best_seq = arr2.copy()
                best_actual = float(sc2)
                best_proxy = float(p2)
                print("NEW BEST sym [" + label + "] score=" + str(sc2) +
                      " n=" + str(arr2.size) +
                      " proxy=" + str(p2))

    return best_seq, best_actual, best_proxy


def propose_candidate(budget_s=1800.0):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    try:
        total_budget = max(60.0, min(float(budget_s) - 95.0, 1700.0))
    except Exception:
        total_budget = 1600.0

    print("Hybrid optimizer started. budget=" + str(round(total_budget, 1)))

    parent = None
    try:
        pv = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if pv is not None:
            p = np.asarray(pv, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
            np.maximum(p, 0.0, out=p)
            if p.size > 0 and float(p.sum()) > 1e-12:
                parent = norm_sum1(p)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    n_main = 7000
    if parent is not None:
        n_main = int(parent.size)
        if n_main < 3000:
            n_main = 4500
        elif n_main > 8500:
            n_main = 7500
    n_main = int(max(2500, min(8500, n_main)))

    n_alt = int(min(9000, n_main + 1300))
    if n_alt == n_main:
        if n_main < 8000:
            n_alt = int(min(9000, n_main + 1000))
        else:
            n_alt = int(max(3000, n_main - 1000))

    if parent is not None:
        a_main = interpolate_sequence(parent, n_main)
    else:
        a_main = default_sine(n_main)

    main_name, main_bool, actual0 = infer_mask(a_main, verbose=True)
    main_idx = mask_to_idx(main_bool, n_main)
    c0 = fft_conv(a_main)
    proxy0 = score_c(c0, n_main, main_idx)

    best_seq = a_main.copy()
    best_actual = float(actual0) if math.isfinite(actual0) else float(proxy0)
    best_proxy = float(proxy0)
    best_name = main_name

    seeds = []
    seeds.append(default_sine(n_main))
    seeds.append(random_smooth(n_main, 101))
    seeds.append(comb_seed(n_main, 0.8, 202))
    if parent is not None:
        seeds.append(symmetrize(interpolate_sequence(parent, n_main)))

    for si, s in enumerate(seeds):
        if time.monotonic() - t_start > total_budget * 0.08:
            break
        arr = norm_sum1(s)
        c = fft_conv(arr)
        p = score_c(c, n_main, main_idx)
        if p < best_proxy - 1e-7:
            best_seq, best_actual, best_proxy = try_candidates(
                [arr], main_idx, best_seq, best_actual, best_proxy, "seed_" + str(si)
            )

    remaining = total_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
    if remaining > 220:
        phase_budget = min(remaining * 0.58, 950.0)
        b_lb = phase_budget * 0.55
        b_fw = phase_budget * 0.30
        b_cp = phase_budget * 0.15

        if b_lb > 30.0:
            print("\n=== Main length L-BFGS n=" + str(n_main) + " budget=" + str(round(b_lb, 1)) + " ===")
            a_lb = lbfegs_schedule(best_seq, b_lb, main_idx, verbose=True)
            best_seq, best_actual, best_proxy = try_candidates(
                [a_lb], main_idx, best_seq, best_actual, best_proxy, "main_lb"
            )

        remaining = total_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
        if b_fw > 30.0 and remaining > 100.0:
            b_fw = min(b_fw, remaining * 0.35)
            print("\n=== Main length FW budget=" + str(round(b_fw, 1)) + " ===")
            a_fw = fw_search(best_seq, b_fw, main_idx, verbose=True)
            best_seq, best_actual, best_proxy = try_candidates(
                [a_fw], main_idx, best_seq, best_actual, best_proxy, "main_fw"
            )

        remaining = total_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
        if b_cp > 30.0 and remaining > 60.0:
            b_cp = min(b_cp, remaining * 0.40)
            print("\n=== Main coordinate budget=" + str(round(b_cp, 1)) + " ===")
            a_cp = coordinate_polish(best_seq, b_cp, main_idx, verbose=True)
            best_seq, best_actual, best_proxy = try_candidates(
                [a_cp], main_idx, best_seq, best_actual, best_proxy, "main_cp"
            )

    remaining = total_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
    if n_alt != n_main and remaining > 330:
        print("\n=== Alt length probe n=" + str(n_alt) + " ===")
        a_alt = interpolate_sequence(best_seq, n_alt)
        alt_name, alt_bool, actual_alt = infer_mask(a_alt, verbose=False)
        alt_idx = mask_to_idx(alt_bool, n_alt)
        c_alt = fft_conv(a_alt)
        proxy_alt = score_c(c_alt, n_alt, alt_idx)

        alt_seq = a_alt.copy()
        alt_actual = float(actual_alt) if math.isfinite(actual_alt) else float(proxy_alt)
        alt_proxy = float(proxy_alt)
        alt_name_out = alt_name

        phase_budget = min(remaining * 0.45, 560.0)
        b_lb = phase_budget * 0.55
        b_fw = phase_budget * 0.30
        b_cp = phase_budget * 0.15

        if b_lb > 30.0:
            a_lb = lbfegs_schedule(alt_seq, b_lb, alt_idx, verbose=True)
            alt_seq, alt_actual, alt_proxy = try_candidates(
                [a_lb], alt_idx, alt_seq, alt_actual, alt_proxy, "alt_lb"
            )

        remaining = total_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
        if b_fw > 30.0 and remaining > 90.0:
            b_fw = min(b_fw, remaining * 0.35)
            a_fw = fw_search(alt_seq, b_fw, alt_idx, verbose=True)
            alt_seq, alt_actual, alt_proxy = try_candidates(
                [a_fw], alt_idx, alt_seq, alt_actual, alt_proxy, "alt_fw"
            )

        remaining = total_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
        if b_cp > 30.0 and remaining > 60.0:
            b_cp = min(b_cp, remaining * 0.40)
            a_cp = coordinate_polish(alt_seq, b_cp, alt_idx, verbose=True)
            alt_seq, alt_actual, alt_proxy = try_candidates(
                [a_cp], alt_idx, alt_seq, alt_actual, alt_proxy, "alt_cp"
            )

        if math.isfinite(alt_actual) and alt_actual < best_actual - 1e-15:
            best_seq = alt_seq.copy()
            best_actual = float(alt_actual)
            best_proxy = float(alt_proxy)
            best_name = alt_name_out
            c = fft_conv(best_seq)
            m = mask_to_idx(mask_from_name(best_seq.size, best_name), best_seq.size)
            best_proxy = score_c(c, best_seq.size, m)
            print("Alt length became global best: score=" + str(best_actual) +
                  " n=" + str(best_seq.size))

    remaining = total_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
    if remaining > 90.0:
        print("\n=== Final global polish ===")
        n = int(best_seq.size)
        final_bool = mask_from_name(n, best_name)
        final_idx = mask_to_idx(final_bool, n)

        b_cp = min(remaining * 0.45, 220.0)
        if b_cp > 30.0:
            a_cp = coordinate_polish(best_seq, b_cp, final_idx, verbose=False)
            best_seq, best_actual, best_proxy = try_candidates(
                [a_cp], final_idx, best_seq, best_actual, best_proxy, "final_cp"
            )

        remaining = total_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
        if SCIPY_OK and remaining > 50.0:
            b_lb = min(remaining * 0.45, 180.0)
            arr = norm_sum1(best_seq)
            c = fft_conv(arr)
            L = 2 * arr.size - 1
            top_count = int(max(60, min(700, L // 25)))
            fixed = make_fixed_idx(c, final_idx, top_count, 0.70)
            cost = estimate_lse_cost(arr, fixed)
            maxfun = int(max(40, min(420, int(b_lb / max(cost * 1.35, 0.1)))))
            for beta in (400.0, 1500.0):
                left = total_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
                if left < 30.0:
                    break
                fixed = make_fixed_idx(fft_conv(best_seq), final_idx, top_count, 0.70)
                a_lb = run_lbfegs(best_seq, beta, fixed, maxfun, verbose=False)
                best_seq, best_actual, best_proxy = try_candidates(
                    [a_lb], final_idx, best_seq, best_actual, best_proxy, "final_lb_" + str(int(beta))
                )

        remaining = total_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_start)
        for i in range(3):
            if remaining < 25.0:
                break
            n = int(best_seq.size)
            final_idx = mask_to_idx(mask_from_name(n, best_name), n)
            a_p = symmetrize(perturb_mul(best_seq, 0.025 + 0.005 * i, seed=3000 + i))
            c = fft_conv(a_p)
            p = score_c(c, n, final_idx)
            if p < best_proxy - 1e-7:
                best_seq, best_actual, best_proxy = try_candidates(
                    [a_p], final_idx, best_seq, best_actual, best_proxy, "final_perturb_" + str(i)
                )

    final_arr = norm_sum1(best_seq)
    final_val = safe_eval(final_arr)
    if not math.isfinite(final_val):
        fallback = default_sine(min(5000, max(1000, int(best_seq.size))))
        final_arr = fallback
        final_val = safe_eval(final_arr)

    if final_val < best_actual - 1e-15:
        best_actual = float(final_val)
        best_seq = final_arr.copy()

    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_start
    arr = np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0, out=arr)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if arr.size == 0 or s < 0.01:
        arr = default_sine(3000)
    else:
        arr = arr / s

    final_val = safe_eval(arr)
    print("\nFINAL returned score=" + str(final_val) +
          " n=" + str(arr.size) +
          " elapsed=" + str(round(elapsed, 1)))

    return [float(x) for x in arr]
```
